# Agents · B · part 9

956 entries, bk-researcher to blue-secretary. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **bk-researcher**: Answers a technical question a skill cannot verify locally, from independent sources and the pinned major's docs, with confidence labels. ([tuyenht/Bearingkit](https://github.com/tuyenht/Bearingkit/blob/HEAD/agents/bk-researcher.md))
- **bk-reviewer**: Independent review of hot-path changes (auth, payments, data deletion, migrations, tenancy) by a non-author. For bk-review and bk-ship. ([tuyenht/Bearingkit](https://github.com/tuyenht/Bearingkit/blob/HEAD/agents/bk-reviewer.md))
- **bk-specify**: Runs the /bk.specify phase for one feature — creates the feature worktree/branch and writes the WHAT/WHY spec from a feature description. Use to start a behavior-kit feature when the orchestrator wants phase-level control. ([fay-i/mermaid-mcp](https://github.com/fay-i/mermaid-mcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bk-specify.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **bk-verify**: Independently verifies a wave gate or integration state — runs suites, boots services, smoke-tests endpoints against the real stack, checks DB schema/migrations — and reports evidence without fixing anything. - **bko.breakout-confirmer**: Breakout confirmation and fakeout filter for day trading. Use when price has triggered a breakout setup to determine if the move is real or a fakeout. Called by bko.orchestrator after bko.setup-monitor flags SETUP FORMING, or directly when the user asks to 'confirm the breakout on [ticker]', 'is this a real breakout or fakeout', or 'should I enter [ticker] now'. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/bko/agents/bko.breakout-confirmer.md))
- **bko.exit-monitor**: Background exit monitor for open breakout day trade positions. Use after entering a breakout trade to watch for exit signals, when the user says 'monitor my [ticker] breakout', 'watch [ticker] for exit', or 'alert me when to exit [ticker] breakout'. Runs non-blocking. Evaluates continuation vs. reversal signals and advises partial or full exits with urgency levels. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/bko/agents/bko.exit-monitor.md))
- **bko.level-mapper**: Key level mapper for breakout day trading. Use to identify and structure the price levels that define a breakout trade for one or more tickers. Invoked twice per session: pre-market (daily levels) and at ~9:45 AM (add Opening Range High/Low). Called by bko.orchestrator or directly when the user asks to 'map levels for [ticker]', 'what are the key levels on [ticker]', or 'mark the breakout levels'. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/bko/agents/bko.level-mapper.md))
- **bko.orchestrator**: Orchestrates the full breakout day trading workflow. Use when starting a breakout trading session, or when the user says 'start breakout session', 'run breakout workflow', 'begin breakout trading day', or 'breakout scan today'. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/bko/agents/bko.orchestrator.md))
- **bko.pre-market-analyst**: Pre-market analyst for breakout day trading. Use before market open when the user asks to 'find breakout candidates', 'scan for gap stocks', 'what's gapping today', or 'pre-market breakout scan'. Produces a ranked candidate list with catalysts and breakout-specific filters to feed into bko.level-mapper. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/bko/agents/bko.pre-market-analyst.md))
- **bko.risk-manager**: Stop-loss and trailing plan advisor for open breakout day trades. Use immediately after entering a breakout trade, when the user asks to 'set my stop for [ticker]', 'manage risk on [ticker] breakout', 'where should my stop be after breakout', or 'trail my stop on [ticker]'. Requires entry price, breakout level, and current price. Outputs a phased stop plan with exact price levels. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/bko/agents/bko.risk-manager.md))
- **bko.setup-monitor**: Intraday breakout pattern monitor for day trading. Use during market hours (9:30-11:00 AM ET) to watch for ORB, Bull Flag, Flat Top, and range compression patterns forming near key levels. Called by bko.orchestrator or directly when the user asks to 'check setup on [ticker]', 'is [ticker] forming a pattern', or 'watch [ticker] for a breakout setup'. ([DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit](https://github.com/DINHDUY/ai-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/workflows/bko/agents/bko.setup-monitor.md))
- **bl-architect**: Dev-team System Architect. Reads the PRD set and design specs, then writes the blueprint (decision records, architecture.md, SECURITY_MODEL.md, Supabase SQL migrations with RLS, docs/openapi.yaml) and scaffolds a repo skeleton that compiles — the single source of truth both developers build against. Answers contract questions. Dispatched by bl-orchestrator only, for one ledger step at a time. ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-architect.md))
- **bl-backend**: Dev-team Back-End Developer. Works in the wt-backend lane implementing every operation in docs/openapi.yaml as thin Next.js route handlers over typed services, on Supabase Postgres with RLS always applied, Supabase Auth and the SECURITY_MODEL permission matrix, idempotent integrations (Stripe, GHL) and background jobs — proven by Vitest integration tests against the staging Supabase branch that check every response against the contract and the 401/403/404 matrix. Dispatched by bl-orchestrator only, for one ledger step at a time. ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-backend.md))
- **bl-builder**: Builds ONE branch of an approved build design, in its own git worktree, requirement by requirement. Station ③ of the buildloop. Works only from the written doc — never from conversation, never from its own judgment about what the doc "meant". Never proves its own work. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-builder.md))
- **bl-designer**: Dev-team UI/UX Designer. Before the build — produces the brand contract (DESIGN.md), docs/user-flows.md, docs/design-specs.md and token-only HTML mockups rendered to screenshots. After the build — reviews the staging preview against its designs and files findings. Dispatched by bl-orchestrator only, for one ledger step at a time. ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-designer.md))
- **bl-devops**: Dev-team DevOps & Deployment. Sets up the Vercel + Supabase environments as code (vercel.json, infra/environments.json, branch-specific previews, preview protection), the GitHub Actions pipelines (CI on every lane and PR, QA smoke on staging deploys, security scans with pinned checksum-verified tools, uptime), environment-variable drift checks (names only), and the production release — after Gate 3 only, through a workflow Matthew approves in GitHub — migrations, prebuilt deploy, read-only smoke, automatic rollback, release log and runbook. Never sees or sets secret values. On an existing pro… ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-devops.md))
- **bl-docs**: Dev-team Documentation & SOP agent. Writes and maintains the human-readable SOPs for whatever the team builds or changes (operator SOPs, company SOPs, agent-team install and redeploy guides, client staff SOPs) and a readable index of the team's agents. Documents only what was built and verified, citing the evidence for every step; marks anything it can't evidence as unverified. Never changes code, settings or production. Dispatched by bl-orchestrator only, for one ledger step at a time (SOP plan in the design phase, SOPs in the verify phase), or for a stand-alone documentation project Matthew… ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-docs.md))
- **bl-fixer**: Fix con causa ignota, invariante, dato di produzione, o che tocca due repo insieme. Apre la PR. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-fixer.md))

- **bl-frontend**: Dev-team Front-End Developer. - **bl-gate**: Reviews a build design against this repo's own laws — its rubric documents, architecture, ownership and the doctrine — BEFORE any code is written. Asks what this writes, and above all what READS it back. Returns APPROVED or a named rejection with the constraints the rewrite must satisfy. Station ②ᵇ of the buildloop. Never rewrites the doc, never edits code. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-gate.md))
- **bl-init-expert**: Git-branchless initialization specialist for setting up repos with branchless workflow ([jpoutrin/product-forge](https://github.com/jpoutrin/product-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/git-workflow/agents/bl-init-expert.md) · ★16)
- **bl-integration**: Dev-team Integration Engineer. Connects client apps to any outside system — GoHighLevel, Stripe, QuickBooks, Loyverse and other POS/ERP/CRM platforms, Monday/HubSpot, email/SMS, file feeds — through typed wrappers with timeouts, safe retries, a database-backed circuit breaker and idempotency; signed, de-duplicated webhooks; cursor-based sync jobs; OAuth and per-tenant tokens in Supabase Vault; outage behaviour users can see; and offline provider simulators QA reuses. Builds exactly what docs/integration-contract.md says. On projects that already have integrations, starts with a read-only take… ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-integration.md))
- **bl-integrator**: Owns the SEAMS between branches of a build — the defects no single branch owner can see. Merges branch worktrees, hunts contradictions between them, and runs the whole-build check no individual builder ran. Station ③ᶜ of the buildloop. Exists because "many authors, no integrator" is the root cause behind an entire class of worst-bug. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-integrator.md))
- **bl-orchestrator**: Lead of the Baseleap Dev & Engineering team. Runs as the main session (`claude --agent bl-orchestrator --permission-mode default`) — turns a stack brief into a gated, dependency-ordered build plan and runs the bl-* specialists through the 5-phase assembly line. Not for use as a subagent. ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-orchestrator.md))
- **bl-planner**: Legge una issue (o un cluster) e il codice, e produce la scheda a 5 campi da far ratificare all'orchestratore. Non implementa. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-planner.md))
- **bl-planner**: Writes the build design through the lens of the profession that actually owns this class of problem. Works out what KIND of problem it is, who does it for a living, how those practitioners approach it, and drafts the full build doc in their idiom using the /buildplan format. Station ② of the buildloop — runs after the audit squad, hands its doc to bl-gate. Writes only the build doc; never touches source. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-planner.md))
- **bl-pm**: Dev-team Product Manager. Turns the brief, intake decisions and client research into docs/PRD.md, docs/user-stories.md and docs/metrics-spec.md — asking Matthew discovery questions through the orchestrator first when anything material is unknown. Dispatched by bl-orchestrator only, for one ledger step at a time. ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-pm.md))
- **bl-prover**: Runs an approved build doc's acceptance checks against what was actually built — from a context that never saw the build session's reasoning. Classifies every result HARNESS-PROVEN or LIVE-PROVEN, hunts the four ways a harness lies, and returns a per-check verdict. Station ③ᵇ of the buildloop. Never fixes what it finds, never edits source. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-prover.md))
- **bl-qa**: Dev-team QA & Testing. Turns the PRD's acceptance criteria into docs/test-plan.md and black-box tests — Playwright user flows, API boundary checks, permission probes across two tenants and a Schemathesis fuzz run — executed on the staging Vercel preview, never locally or in production. Reports pass/fail against every criterion and writes one root-cause bug ticket per failure for the orchestrator to route. Read-only on application code; never fixes it, never weakens a test. Dispatched by bl-orchestrator only, for one ledger step at a time. ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-qa.md))
- **bl-reviewer**: Dev-team Code Reviewer. Independently reviews the staging diff against the PRD acceptance criteria, the API contract and the architecture — correctness, contract conformance, minimal diffs, dead/duplicated code, strict typing, test honesty, N+1 and unbounded queries, race conditions and missing idempotency — and writes a severity-ranked APPROVED/BLOCKED verdict with routable patch directives. Read-only on code; never edits it. Runs in parallel with bl-security. Dispatched by bl-orchestrator only, for one ledger step at a time. ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-reviewer.md))
- **bl-rootcause**: Takes a list of symptoms and collapses them into the smallest set of real defects. Exists because four separate red findings once turned out to be ONE bug, and because a retrieval problem got four wrong diagnoses before anyone found the corrupt index underneath all of them. Station ① of the buildloop, after bl-scout and bl-runtime report. Never edits. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-rootcause.md))
- **bl-runtime**: Read-only reality check on what is ACTUALLY running. Compares the config file against the RUNNING process environment, checks service state, deployed version drift, and data-store stats. Exists because config in a file is not config in a process. Station ① of the buildloop. Never restarts, never writes. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-runtime.md))
- **bl-scout**: Rimisura la claim di una issue del backlog frontaliereticino. Conta, legge un artifact, verifica se il difetto esiste ancora. Non modifica nulla. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-scout.md))
- **bl-scout**: Read-only mapper for a codebase. Answers "where does X live", "what actually runs on the live path", "what's already been tried" — without polluting the main session's context. - **blocking-wait-cpu-accounting**: Kernel blocking syscalls (poll, nanosleep, read) use an `sti; hlt` loop to wait for events. The process remains `rq.curr()` — it is never dequeued via `block_current()`. The timer ISR fires every 10ms (TICK_NS) on all CPUs and `scheduler_tick()` charges `sum_exec_runtime += TICK_NS` for the current task. ([chris17453/oxide_os](https://github.com/chris17453/oxide_os/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/blocking-wait-cpu-accounting.md) · ★3)
